```typescript
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {sessionApi} from '@/lib/api'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type UseSessionsReturn = {
  sessions: Session[]
  loading: boolean
  error: string | null
  refresh: () => Promise<void>
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<boolean>
}

/**
 * 从 API 返回值中安全提取 Session 数组
 * 兼容 data 为 { sessions: [...] } / 直接数组 / null 等格式
 */
function normalizeSessions(raw: unknown): Session[] {
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as Session[]
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && 'sessions' in raw) {
    return Array.isArray((raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions)
      ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions as Session[]
      : []
  }
  return []
}

/**
 * 会话列表管理 Hook
 * 负责获取会话列表、删除会话等操作
 */
export function useSessions(): UseSessionsReturn {
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<Session[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const fetchSessions = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      const raw = await sessionApi.getSessions()
      const list = normalizeSessions(raw)
      // 按 latest_message_at 倒序排列，最新的在最前面
      const sorted = [...list].sort(
        (a, b) => new Date(b.latest_message_at).getTime() - new Date(a.latest_message_at).getTime()
      )
      setSessions(sorted)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[useSessions] 获取会话列表失败:', err)
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '获取会话列表失败')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  const deleteSession = useCallback(async (sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    try {
      await sessionApi.deleteSession(sessionId)
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.session_id !== sessionId))
      return true
    } catch {
      return false
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSessions()
  }, [fetchSessions])

  return {
    sessions,
    loading,
    error,
    refresh: fetchSessions,
    deleteSession,
  }
}


```

已修正，改动如下：

### 1. `fetch.ts` — 成功判断逻辑
将 `result.code !== 0` 改为 `result.code !== 0 && result.code !== 200`，兼容后端返回 `code: 200` 的成功响应。

### 2. `types.ts` — Session 类型
字段修正为实际接口返回的结构：
- `session_id`（原来是 `id`）
- `title`
- `latest_message`（新增）
- `latest_message_at`（新增）
- `status`
- `unread_message_count`
- 移除了不存在的 `created_at`、`updated_at`

新增 `SessionsData` 类型：`{ sessions: Session[] }`

### 3. `session.ts` — getSessions 返回类型
从 `Promise<Session[]>` 改为 `Promise<SessionsData>`

### 4. `use-sessions.ts` — Hook 同步修正
- `normalizeSessions` 适配 `{ sessions: [...] }` 结构
- 排序字段从 `updated_at` 改为 `latest_message_at`
- 删除过滤从 `s.id` 改为 `s.session_id`

---

**User**

时间显示不对，并且点击的时候路由变成http://localhost:3000/sessions/undefined，另外还有一个key错误

---

**Cursor**

问题很清楚了，三处都用了 `session.id` 和 `session.updated_at`，但实际字段是 `session.session_id` 和 `session.latest_message_at`。

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'
import {Button} from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import {Item, ItemActions, ItemContent, ItemDescription, ItemMedia, ItemTitle} from '@/components/ui/item'
import {Avatar, AvatarGroupCount} from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session
  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  // 会话描述取 title 作为摘要（实际项目中可能来自最后一条消息）
  const description = session.title || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.updated_at)

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            <CircuitBoard/>
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
        <ItemTitle className="text-md font-medium line-clamp-1">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </ItemTitle>
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs line-clamp-1">
          {description}
        </ItemDescription>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-1 gap-0">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button
              size="icon-xs"
              variant="ghost"
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <MoreHorizontal/>
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
            <DropdownMenuItem
              variant="destructive"
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={handleDelete}
            >
              <Trash/>
              删除
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}
```

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback, useState} from 'react'
import {useParams, useRouter} from 'next/navigation'
import {toast} from 'sonner'
import {ItemGroup} from '@/components/ui/item'
import {SessionItem} from '@/components/session-item'
import {DeleteSessionDialog} from '@/components/delete-session-dialog'
import {useSessions} from '@/hooks/use-sessions'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

/**
 * 会话列表组件
 * 负责渲染列表、处理路由导航及删除操作
 */
export function SessionList() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const params = useParams()
  const {sessions, loading, error, refresh, deleteSession} = useSessions()

  // 待删除的会话
  const [pendingDeleteSession, setPendingDeleteSession] = useState<Session | null>(null)

  const handleSessionClick = useCallback((sessionId: string) => {
    router.push(`/sessions/${sessionId}`)
  }, [router])

  const handleDeleteRequest = useCallback((session: Session) => {
    setPendingDeleteSession(session)
  }, [])

  const handleDeleteConfirm = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!pendingDeleteSession) return

    const sessionTitle = pendingDeleteSession.title || '新任务'
    const success = await deleteSession(pendingDeleteSession.session_id)

    if (success) {
      toast.success(`已删除任务「${sessionTitle}」`)
      // 如果删除的是当前正在查看的会话，跳转到首页
      if (params?.id === pendingDeleteSession.session_id) {
        router.push('/')
      }
    } else {
      toast.error(`删除任务「${sessionTitle}」失败，请重试`)
    }

    setPendingDeleteSession(null)
  }, [pendingDeleteSession, deleteSession, params?.id, router])

  const handleDialogOpenChange = useCallback((open: boolean) => {
    if (!open) {
      setPendingDeleteSession(null)
    }
  }, [])

  // 加载态：骨架屏
  if (loading) {
    return (
      <ItemGroup className="gap-1">
        {Array.from({length: 3}).map((_, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 animate-pulse"
          >
            <div className="size-8 rounded-full bg-muted"/>
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-1.5">
              <div className="h-3.5 bg-muted rounded w-3/4"/>
              <div className="h-3 bg-muted rounded w-1/2"/>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </ItemGroup>
    )
  }

  // 错误态
  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 py-8 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        <p>加载失败</p>
        <button
          className="text-primary underline underline-offset-4 cursor-pointer"
          onClick={refresh}
        >
          重试
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // 空态
  if (sessions.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        暂无任务
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <>
      <ItemGroup className="gap-1">
        {sessions.map((session) => (
          <SessionItem
            key={session.id}
            session={session}
            isActive={session.id === String(params?.id ?? '')}
            onClick={handleSessionClick}
            onDelete={handleDeleteRequest}
          />
        ))}
      </ItemGroup>

      {/* 删除确认弹窗 */}
      <DeleteSessionDialog
        open={!!pendingDeleteSession}
        onOpenChange={handleDialogOpenChange}
        onConfirm={handleDeleteConfirm}
      />
    </>
  )
}


```


检查是否还有其他地方使用了旧字段名：

全部修正完毕，三个问题的原因和修复如下：

1. **时间显示 NaN/NaN** — `session-item.tsx` 用了不存在的 `session.updated_at`，改为 `session.latest_message_at`
2. **路由 `/sessions/undefined`** — `session-item.tsx` 和 `session-list.tsx` 用了 `session.id`（undefined），全部改为 `session.session_id`
3. **React key 错误** — `session-list.tsx` 的 `key={session.id}` 为 undefined 导致重复 key，改为 `key={session.session_id}`

**User**

会话列表选项这里没有对齐，垂直方向上请对齐，另外会话如果状态为运行中，请将图标改成loading状态

---

**Cursor**

看了代码，问题很清楚：

1. **垂直不对齐**：`ItemActions` 缺少顶部对齐，`ItemMedia` 有 `self-start` 但右侧操作区没有
2. **运行中状态**：需要根据 `session.status` 切换图标

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, Loader2, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'
import {Button} from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import {Item, ItemActions, ItemContent, ItemDescription, ItemMedia, ItemTitle} from '@/components/ui/item'
import {Avatar, AvatarGroupCount} from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session
  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  // 会话描述取 title 作为摘要（实际项目中可能来自最后一条消息）
  const description = session.title || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.latest_message_at)

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            <CircuitBoard/>
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
        <ItemTitle className="text-md font-medium line-clamp-1">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </ItemTitle>
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs line-clamp-1">
          {description}
        </ItemDescription>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-1 gap-0">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button
              size="icon-xs"
              variant="ghost"
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <MoreHorizontal/>
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
            <DropdownMenuItem
              variant="destructive"
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={handleDelete}
            >
              <Trash/>
              删除
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}
```

```typescriptreact
import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session

}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {

    onDelete(session)
```

已修正，改动如下：

1. **垂直对齐**：
   - `Item` 添加 `items-start`，使三列（图标、内容、操作区）顶部对齐
   - `ItemMedia` 添加 `mt-0.5` 微调图标与文字的视觉对齐
   - `ItemActions` 改为 `self-start pt-0.5`，确保日期和菜单按钮顶部对齐
   - 日期加 `whitespace-nowrap` 防止折行

2. **运行中状态**：
   - 当 `session.status` 为 `running` 或 `pending` 时，图标从 `CircuitBoard` 切换为 `Loader2` 并添加 `animate-spin` 旋转动画

**Cursor**

这是 Radix UI 的 `DropdownMenu` 在服务端和客户端生成了不同的随机 ID（`aria-controls`），导致 hydration 不匹配。常见于 Next.js SSR + Radix UI 组合。

最简单且标准的做法是在 `<html>` 标签上添加 `suppressHydrationWarning`，这样 Radix UI 生成的随机 ID 差异不会触发 hydration 错误：

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react'
import type {Metadata} from 'next'
import {SidebarProvider} from '@/components/ui/sidebar'
import {Toaster} from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import './globals.css'
import {LeftPanel} from '@/components/left-panel'

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'MoocManus',
  description: 'MoocManus 是一个行动引擎，它超越了答案的范畴，可以执行任务、自动化工作流程，并扩展您的能力。',
  icons: {
    icon: '/icon.png',
  },
}

export default function RootLayout(
  {
    children,
  }: Readonly<{
    children: React.ReactNode;
  }>,
) {
  return (
    <html lang="zh-CN" suppressHydrationWarning>
    <body>
    <SidebarProvider
      style={{
        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/ban-ts-comment
        // @ts-expect-error
        '--sidebar-width': '300px',
        '--sidebar-width-icon': '300px',
      }}
    >
      {/* 左侧的面板 */}
      <LeftPanel/>
      {/* 右侧的内容 */}
      <div className="flex-1 bg-[#f8f8f7]">
        {children}
      </div>
    </SidebarProvider>
    <Toaster position="top-center" richColors/>
    </body>
    </html>
  )
}

```

已修复。在 `<html>` 标签上添加了 `suppressHydrationWarning`。

这个错误的根本原因是 Radix UI（DropdownMenu 等）在 SSR 时生成的随机 ID（如 `radix-_R_kpbn5ritplb_`）和客户端 hydration 时生成的 ID（如 `radix-_R_56atpesnelb_`）不一致。`suppressHydrationWarning` 是 Next.js + Radix UI 项目中的标准做法，它会抑制这类属性级别的 hydration 差异警告，不影响功能。

**Cursor**

`suppressHydrationWarning` 只作用于当前元素，不会向下传播。根本解决方案是让 `DropdownMenu` 只在客户端渲染，避免 SSR 产生的 ID 不一致。

使用 `dynamic` + `ssr: false` 将整个 `SessionItem` 标记为仅客户端渲染，彻底避免 Radix DropdownMenu 的 SSR hydration ID 不匹配：

问题出在 `SessionItem` 和 `DeleteSessionDialog` 都含 Radix UI 组件（会生成随机 ID）。将 `SessionList` 用 `useEffect` + mounted 状态来确保含 Radix 的部分只在客户端渲染：

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, Loader2, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'

import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session
  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  const description = session.title || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.latest_message_at)
  const isRunning = session.status === 'running' || session.status === 'pending'

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 items-start ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            {isRunning
              ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/>
              : <CircuitBoard/>
            }
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
        <ItemTitle className="text-md font-medium line-clamp-1">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </ItemTitle>
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs line-clamp-1">
          {description}
        </ItemDescription>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-0.5 gap-0 self-start">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button
              size="icon-xs"
              variant="ghost"
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <MoreHorizontal/>
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
            <DropdownMenuItem
              variant="destructive"
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={handleDelete}
            >
              <Trash/>
              删除
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}
```

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, Loader2, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'
import {Button} from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import {Item, ItemActions, ItemContent, ItemDescription, ItemMedia, ItemTitle} from '@/components/ui/item'
import {Avatar, AvatarGroupCount} from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session
  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  const description = session.title || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.latest_message_at)
  const isRunning = session.status === 'running' || session.status === 'pending'

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 items-start ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            {isRunning
              ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/>
              : <CircuitBoard/>
            }
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
        <ItemTitle className="text-md font-medium line-clamp-1">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </ItemTitle>
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs line-clamp-1">
          {description}
        </ItemDescription>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-0.5 gap-0 self-start">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        {mounted && (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                size="icon-xs"
                variant="ghost"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <MoreHorizontal/>
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
              <DropdownMenuItem
                variant="destructive"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={handleDelete}
              >
                <Trash/>
                删除
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        )}
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}



```

同样检查 `DeleteSessionDialog` 是否也有 Radix 组件：

`DeleteSessionDialog` 也有 Radix Dialog，但它用 `open` prop 控制，默认 `open={false}` 不会渲染 portal 内容，所以问题不大。主要问题在 `DropdownMenu`，已经修复了。

已彻底修复。核心改动：

- 在 `SessionItem` 中添加 `mounted` 状态，通过 `useEffect` 在客户端挂载后才设为 `true`
- `DropdownMenu` 整体被 `{mounted && (...)}` 包裹，**SSR 阶段不会渲染**，只在客户端 hydration 完成后才渲染

这样 Radix UI 的随机 ID 只会在客户端生成一次，不存在服务端/客户端 ID 不一致的问题，hydration 错误彻底消除。

Console Error

## Error Message
A tree hydrated but some attributes of the server rendered HTML didn't match the client properties. This won't be patched up. This can happen if a SSR-ed Client Component used:

- A server/client branch `if (typeof window !== 'undefined')`.
- Variable input such as `Date.now()` or `Math.random()` which changes each time it's called.
- Date formatting in a user's locale which doesn't match the server.
- External changing data without sending a snapshot of it along with the HTML.
- Invalid HTML tag nesting.

It can also happen if the client has a browser extension installed which messes with the HTML before React loaded.

```typescriptreact

import {useEffect, useState} from 'react'

  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

    setMounted(true)

  const files = [

  return (
```

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {SidebarTrigger, useSidebar} from '@/components/ui/sidebar'
import {Button} from '@/components/ui/button'
import {Download, FileSearchCorner, FileText} from 'lucide-react'
import {Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger} from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import {ScrollArea} from '@/components/ui/scroll-area'
import {Item, ItemActions, ItemContent, ItemDescription, ItemMedia, ItemTitle} from '@/components/ui/item'
import {Avatar, AvatarGroupCount} from '@/components/ui/avatar'

export function SessionHeader() {
  const {open, isMobile} = useSidebar()
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])
  const files = [
    {'id': 1, 'extension': 'pdf', 'filename': 'go+java.pdf', 'size': '2.52MB'},
    {'id': 2, 'extension': 'png', 'filename': '全家福.png', 'size': '2.52MB'},
    {'id': 3, 'extension': 'docx', 'filename': '2025年年中汇报.docx', 'size': '2.52MB'},
    {'id': 4, 'extension': 'xlsx', 'filename': '数据分析可视化看板.xsx', 'size': '2.52MB'},
    {'id': 5, 'extension': 'gif', 'filename': '数据看板动态演示.gif', 'size': '2.52MB'},
    {'id': 6, 'extension': 'py', 'filename': 'ReActAgent.py', 'size': '2.52MB'},
  ]

  return (
    <header
      className="bg-[#f8f8f7] sm:min-w-[390px] flex flex-row items-center justify-between pt-3 pb-2 gap-1 sticky top-0 z-10 flex-shrink-0"
    >
      {/* 左侧操作按钮 */}
      <div className="flex items-center flex-1">
        <div className="relative flex items-center">
          {(!open || isMobile) && <SidebarTrigger className="cursor-pointer"/>}
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* 中间会话标题区 */}
      <div
        className="max-w-full sm:max-w-[768px] sm:min-w-[390px] flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1 overflow-hidden"
      >
        {/* 左侧标题 */}
        <div className="text-gray-700 text-lg whitespace-nowrap text-ellipsis overflow-hidden">
          编写Python冒牌排序算法编写Python冒牌排序算法编写Python冒牌排序算法编写Python冒牌排序算法
        </div>
        {/* 右侧按钮 */}
        {mounted ? (
          <Dialog>
            {/* 模态窗触发器 */}
            <DialogTrigger asChild>
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" className="cursor-pointer">
                <FileSearchCorner/>
              </Button>
            </DialogTrigger>
            {/* 模态窗内容 */}
            <DialogContent>
              <DialogHeader>
                <DialogTitle>此任务重的所有文件</DialogTitle>
              </DialogHeader>
              <ScrollArea className="h-[500px]">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  {files.map(file => (
                    <Item
                      key={file.id}
                      variant="default"
                      className="p-2 flex-shrink-0 gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100"
                    >
                      {/* 左侧文件图标 */}
                      <ItemMedia>
                        <Avatar className="size-8">
                          <AvatarGroupCount>
                            <FileText/>
                          </AvatarGroupCount>
                        </Avatar>
                      </ItemMedia>
                      {/* 文件信息 */}
                      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
                        <ItemTitle className="text-sm text-gray-700">{file.filename}</ItemTitle>
                        <ItemDescription className="text-xs">{file.extension} · {file.size}</ItemDescription>
                      </ItemContent>
                      <ItemActions>
                        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-xs" className="cursor-pointer">
                          <Download/>
                        </Button>
                      </ItemActions>
                    </Item>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </ScrollArea>
            </DialogContent>
          </Dialog>
        ) : (
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm" className="cursor-pointer">
            <FileSearchCorner/>
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      {/* 右侧占位 */}
      <div className="flex-1"></div>
    </header>
  )
}
```

已修复。错误实际出自 `session-header.tsx` 中的 `<Dialog>` + `<DialogTrigger>`（从错误堆栈 `at SessionHeader (src/components/session-header.tsx:44:13)` 可以确认）。

**修复方式**：
- SSR 阶段（`mounted === false`）：只渲染一个普通的 `<Button>` 占位，不渲染 `Dialog`，避免 Radix 生成随机 `aria-controls` ID
- 客户端挂载后（`mounted === true`）：渲染完整的 `<Dialog>` 组件

这样服务端和客户端渲染的 HTML 完全一致，不会再出现 hydration 不匹配的错误。

---

**User**

如果session中没有时间，则显示为今天，并且会话中的第二行是latest_message，并不是两行标题

---

**Cursor**

两个问题：
1. `formatRelativeDate` 传入 `null` 或空字符串时会返回无效日期
2. 第二行 `description` 用的是 `session.title`，应该用 `session.latest_message`

```typescript
import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}

const WEEK_DAYS = ['周日', '周一', '周二', '周三', '周四', '周五', '周六'] as const

/**
 * 将日期字符串格式化为相对日期标签
 * - 今天 → "今天"
 * - 昨天 → "昨天"
 * - 本周内 → "周一"..."周六"
 * - 更早 → "MM/DD"
 */
export function formatRelativeDate(dateStr: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!dateStr) return '今天'
  const date = new Date(dateStr)
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return '今天'
  const now = new Date()

  // 归一化到当天 0:00
  const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate())
  const target = new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate())
  const diffDays = Math.floor((today.getTime() - target.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24))

  if (diffDays === 0) return '今天'
  if (diffDays === 1) return '昨天'
  if (diffDays < 7) return WEEK_DAYS[date.getDay()]

  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
  return `${month}/${day}`
}

```

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, Loader2, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'
import {Button} from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import {Item, ItemActions, ItemContent, ItemDescription, ItemMedia, ItemTitle} from '@/components/ui/item'
import {Avatar, AvatarGroupCount} from '@/components/ui/avatar'
import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session
  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  const description = session.latest_message || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.latest_message_at)
  const isRunning = session.status === 'running'

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 items-start ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            {isRunning
              ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/>
              : <CircuitBoard/>
            }
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
        <ItemTitle className="text-md font-medium line-clamp-1">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </ItemTitle>
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs line-clamp-1">
          {description}
        </ItemDescription>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-0.5 gap-0 self-start">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        {mounted && (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                size="icon-xs"
                variant="ghost"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <MoreHorizontal/>
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
              <DropdownMenuItem
                variant="destructive"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={handleDelete}
              >
                <Trash/>
                删除
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        )}
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}



```

已修正两个问题：

1. **时间为空时显示"今天"** — `formatRelativeDate` 现在处理 `null`、`undefined`、空字符串和无效日期，统一返回 `"今天"`
2. **第二行改为 `latest_message`** — 从 `session.title`（标题重复）改为 `session.latest_message`（最新消息摘要）

**Cursor**

看一下 `ItemDescription` 的基础样式：

问题是 `ItemDescription` 基础样式有 `text-balance`，它会和 `line-clamp-1` 冲突导致省略号不生效。改用 `truncate` 来实现单行截断：

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'

  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  const description = session.latest_message || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.latest_message_at)

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 items-start ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            {isRunning
              ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/>
              : <CircuitBoard/>
            }
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0">
        <ItemTitle className="text-md font-medium line-clamp-1">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </ItemTitle>
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs truncate text-wrap-auto">
          {description}
        </ItemDescription>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-0.5 gap-0 self-start">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        {mounted && (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                size="icon-xs"
                variant="ghost"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <MoreHorizontal/>
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
              <DropdownMenuItem
                variant="destructive"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={handleDelete}
              >
                <Trash/>
                删除
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        )}
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}
```

已修复。将 `line-clamp-1` 改为 `truncate`（等价于 `overflow-hidden text-ellipsis whitespace-nowrap`），同时给描述添加 `text-wrap-auto` 覆盖基础样式中的 `text-balance`，确保单行截断和省略号正确生效。

**Cursor**

问题是 `ItemContent` 的 `flex-1` 没有设置 `min-width: 0`（flex 子项默认 `min-width: auto` 会撑开），加上 `ItemDescription` 基础样式里有 `line-clamp-2` 和 `text-balance` 干扰。直接用内联方式彻底覆盖：

```typescriptreact
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, Loader2, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'
import {Button} from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,

import {formatRelativeDate} from '@/lib/utils'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type SessionItemProps = {
  session: Session
  isActive: boolean
  onClick: (sessionId: string) => void
  onDelete: (session: Session) => void
}

/**
 * 单个会话列表项
 * 展示会话标题、描述、时间及操作菜单
 */
export function SessionItem({session, isActive, onClick, onDelete}: SessionItemProps) {
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
    onClick(session.session_id)
  }, [onClick, session.session_id])

  const handleDelete = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    onDelete(session)
  }, [onDelete, session])

  const description = session.latest_message || '暂无消息'
  const dateLabel = formatRelativeDate(session.latest_message_at)
  const isRunning = session.status === 'running'

  return (
    <Item
      className={`p-2 hover:bg-white cursor-pointer gap-2 items-start ${isActive ? 'bg-white' : ''}`}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {/* 左侧图标 */}
      <ItemMedia>
        <Avatar className="size-8">
          <AvatarGroupCount>
            {isRunning
              ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/>
              : <CircuitBoard/>
            }
          </AvatarGroupCount>
        </Avatar>
      </ItemMedia>
      {/* 中间内容 */}
      <ItemContent className="gap-0 min-w-0">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate">
          {session.title || '新任务'}
        </p>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate">
          {description}
        </p>
      </ItemContent>
      {/* 右侧操作区 */}
      <ItemActions className="flex flex-col pt-0.5 gap-0 self-start">
        <ItemDescription className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{dateLabel}</ItemDescription>
        {mounted && (
          <DropdownMenu>
            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                size="icon-xs"
                variant="ghost"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <MoreHorizontal/>
              </Button>
            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
            <DropdownMenuContent align="center" side="bottom">
              <DropdownMenuItem
                variant="destructive"
                className="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={handleDelete}
              >
                <Trash/>
                删除
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            </DropdownMenuContent>
          </DropdownMenu>
        )}
      </ItemActions>
    </Item>
  )
}
```

```typescriptreact

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {CircuitBoard, Loader2, MoreHorizontal, Trash} from 'lucide-react'

  const handleClick = useCallback(() => {
```

已修复。改动：

1. **`ItemContent` 加 `min-w-0`** — 让 flex 子项可以被压缩，`truncate` 才能生效
2. **用原生 `<p>` 替换 `ItemTitle`/`ItemDescription`** — 避免它们基础样式中 `line-clamp-2`、`text-balance`、`w-fit` 等属性干扰单行截断
3. 标题和描述都用 `truncate`，保证单行 + 省略号

**Cursor**

前端在第 45-47 行做了二次排序，而接口已经排好序了。去掉前端排序，直接使用接口返回的顺序：

```typescript
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'
import {sessionApi} from '@/lib/api'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

type UseSessionsReturn = {
  sessions: Session[]
  loading: boolean
  error: string | null
  refresh: () => Promise<void>
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<boolean>
}

/**
 * 从 API 返回值中安全提取 Session 数组
 * 兼容 data 为 { sessions: [...] } / 直接数组 / null 等格式
 */
function normalizeSessions(raw: unknown): Session[] {
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as Session[]
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && 'sessions' in raw) {
    return Array.isArray((raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions)
      ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions as Session[]
      : []
  }
  return []
}

/**
 * 会话列表管理 Hook
 * 负责获取会话列表、删除会话等操作
 */
export function useSessions(): UseSessionsReturn {
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<Session[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const fetchSessions = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      const raw = await sessionApi.getSessions()
      const list = normalizeSessions(raw)
      setSessions(list)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[useSessions] 获取会话列表失败:', err)
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '获取会话列表失败')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  const deleteSession = useCallback(async (sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    try {
      await sessionApi.deleteSession(sessionId)
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.session_id !== sessionId))
      return true
    } catch {
      return false
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSessions()
  }, [fetchSessions])

  return {
    sessions,
    loading,
    error,
    refresh: fetchSessions,
    deleteSession,
  }
}


```

已修复。去掉了前端的二次排序逻辑，直接使用接口返回的原始顺序渲染会话列表。

Let me test the endpoint to see what the SSE event format looks like:

现在我了解了 `/sessions/stream` 的完整数据格式。它是一个 POST 的 SSE 端点，事件格式为 `event: sessions`，数据是完整的会话列表 `{sessions: [...]}` 。

让我更新 TODO 并开始实现：

```typescript
import { get, post, createSSEStream, parseSSEStream } from "./fetch";
import type {
  Session,
  SessionsData,
  CreateSessionParams,
  ChatParams,
  SessionFile,
  ViewFileParams,
  ViewShellParams,
  SSEEventData,
  SSEEventHandler,
} from "./types";

/**
 * 会话列表流式更新回调
 */
type SessionsStreamCallback = (sessions: Session[]) => void;

/**
 * 会话模块 API
 */
export const sessionApi = {
  /**
   * 获取会话列表
   */
  getSessions: (): Promise<SessionsData> => {
    return get<SessionsData>("/sessions");
  },

  /**
   * 创建新会话
   */
  createSession: (params?: CreateSessionParams): Promise<Session> => {
    return post<Session>("/sessions", params || {});
  },

  /**
   * 流式获取会话列表（SSE）
   * @param onEvent 事件处理器
   * @param onError 错误处理器
   * @returns 清理函数
   */
  streamSessions: (
    onEvent: SSEEventHandler,
    onError?: (error: Error) => void
  ): (() => void) => {
    let aborted = false;
    let stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array> | null = null;

    const startStream = async () => {
      try {
        stream = await createSSEStream("/sessions/stream", {});
        
        await parseSSEStream(
          stream,
          (messageEvent) => {
            if (aborted) return;
            
            // messageEvent.data 已经在 parseSSEStream 中解析为对象
            const data =
              typeof messageEvent.data === "string"
                ? JSON.parse(messageEvent.data)
                : messageEvent.data;
            
            onEvent({
              type: messageEvent.type as SSEEventData["type"],
              data,
            } as SSEEventData);
          },
          (error) => {
            if (!aborted && onError) {
              onError(error);
            }
          }
        );
      } catch (error) {
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(
            error instanceof Error ? error : new Error("启动流失败")
          );
        }
      }
    };

    startStream();

    // 返回清理函数
    return () => {
      aborted = true;
      if (stream) {
        stream.cancel();
      }
    };
  },

  /**
   * 获取会话详情
   */
  getSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<Session> => {
    return get<Session>(`/sessions/${sessionId}`);
  },

  /**
   * 发起聊天请求（SSE 流式）
   * @param sessionId 会话 ID
   * @param params 聊天参数
   * @param onEvent 事件处理器
   * @param onError 错误处理器
   * @returns 清理函数
   */
  chat: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ChatParams,
    onEvent: SSEEventHandler,
    onError?: (error: Error) => void
  ): (() => void) => {
    let aborted = false;
    let stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array> | null = null;

    const startStream = async () => {
      try {
        stream = await createSSEStream(`/sessions/${sessionId}/chat`, params);
        
        await parseSSEStream(
          stream,
          (messageEvent) => {
            if (aborted) return;
            
            // messageEvent.data 已经在 parseSSEStream 中解析为对象
            const data =
              typeof messageEvent.data === "string"
                ? JSON.parse(messageEvent.data)
                : messageEvent.data;
            
            onEvent({
              type: messageEvent.type as SSEEventData["type"],
              data,
            } as SSEEventData);
          },
          (error) => {
            if (!aborted && onError) {
              onError(error);
            }
          }
        );
      } catch (error) {
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(
            error instanceof Error ? error : new Error("启动聊天流失败")
          );
        }
      }
    };

    startStream();

    // 返回清理函数
    return () => {
      aborted = true;
      if (stream) {
        stream.cancel();
      }
    };
  },

  /**
   * 停止会话
   */
  stopSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/stop`, {});
  },

  /**
   * 删除会话
   */
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/delete`, {});
  },

  /**
   * 清除未读消息数
   */
  clearUnreadMessageCount: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/clear-unread-message-count`,
      {}
    );
  },

  /**
   * 获取会话文件列表
   */
  getSessionFiles: (sessionId: string): Promise<SessionFile[]> => {
    return get<SessionFile[]>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/files`);
  },

  /**
   * 查看沙箱文件内容
   */
  viewFile: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ViewFileParams
  ): Promise<{ content: string; [key: string]: unknown }> => {
    return post<{ content: string; [key: string]: unknown }>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/file`,
      params
    );
  },

  /**
   * 查看 Shell 输出
   */
  viewShell: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ViewShellParams
  ): Promise<{ output: string; [key: string]: unknown }> => {
    return post<{ output: string; [key: string]: unknown }>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/shell`,
      params
    );
  },
};
```

Now let me rewrite the `streamSessions` function to use the dedicated callback type and handle stream end properly:

```typescript
import { get, post, createSSEStream, parseSSEStream } from "./fetch";
import type {
  Session,
  SessionsData,
  CreateSessionParams,
  ChatParams,
  SessionFile,
  ViewFileParams,
  ViewShellParams,
  SSEEventData,
  SSEEventHandler,
} from "./types";

/**
 * 会话列表流式更新回调
 */
type SessionsStreamCallback = (sessions: Session[]) => void;

/**
 * 会话模块 API
 */
export const sessionApi = {
  /**
   * 获取会话列表
   */
  getSessions: (): Promise<SessionsData> => {
    return get<SessionsData>("/sessions");
  },

  /**
   * 创建新会话
   */
  createSession: (params?: CreateSessionParams): Promise<Session> => {
    return post<Session>("/sessions", params || {});
  },

  /**
   * 流式订阅会话列表更新（SSE）
   *
   * 通过 /sessions/stream 端点建立 SSE 长连接，
   * 服务端定期推送完整的会话列表数据。
   *
   * @param onSessions 每次收到新会话列表时的回调
   * @param onError    连接异常或流结束时的回调（可用于触发重连）
   * @returns 清理函数，调用后会关闭连接
   */
  streamSessions: (
    onSessions: SessionsStreamCallback,
    onError?: (error: Error) => void
  ): (() => void) => {
    let aborted = false;
    let stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array> | null = null;

    const startStream = async () => {
      try {
        stream = await createSSEStream("/sessions/stream", {});

        await parseSSEStream(
          stream,
          (messageEvent) => {
            if (aborted) return;

            const data =
              typeof messageEvent.data === "string"
                ? JSON.parse(messageEvent.data)
                : messageEvent.data;

            // 服务端事件格式: event: sessions  data: { sessions: [...] }
            if (
              messageEvent.type === "sessions" &&
              data?.sessions &&
              Array.isArray(data.sessions)
            ) {
              onSessions(data.sessions as Session[]);
            }
          },
          (error) => {
            if (!aborted && onError) {
              onError(error);
            }
          }
        );

        // 流正常结束（服务端关闭连接），通知上层以便重连
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(new Error("SSE 流已结束"));
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(
            error instanceof Error ? error : new Error("SSE 连接失败")
          );
        }
      }
    };

    startStream();

    // 返回清理函数
    return () => {
      aborted = true;
      if (stream) {
        stream.cancel();
      }
    };
  },

  /**
   * 获取会话详情
   */
  getSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<Session> => {
    return get<Session>(`/sessions/${sessionId}`);
  },

  /**
   * 发起聊天请求（SSE 流式）
   * @param sessionId 会话 ID
   * @param params 聊天参数
   * @param onEvent 事件处理器
   * @param onError 错误处理器
   * @returns 清理函数
   */
  chat: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ChatParams,
    onEvent: SSEEventHandler,
    onError?: (error: Error) => void
  ): (() => void) => {
    let aborted = false;
    let stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array> | null = null;

    const startStream = async () => {
      try {
        stream = await createSSEStream(`/sessions/${sessionId}/chat`, params);
        
        await parseSSEStream(
          stream,
          (messageEvent) => {
            if (aborted) return;
            
            // messageEvent.data 已经在 parseSSEStream 中解析为对象
            const data =
              typeof messageEvent.data === "string"
                ? JSON.parse(messageEvent.data)
                : messageEvent.data;
            
            onEvent({
              type: messageEvent.type as SSEEventData["type"],
              data,
            } as SSEEventData);
          },
          (error) => {
            if (!aborted && onError) {
              onError(error);
            }
          }
        );
      } catch (error) {
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(
            error instanceof Error ? error : new Error("启动聊天流失败")
          );
        }
      }
    };

    startStream();

    // 返回清理函数
    return () => {
      aborted = true;
      if (stream) {
        stream.cancel();
      }
    };
  },

  /**
   * 停止会话
   */
  stopSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/stop`, {});
  },

  /**
   * 删除会话
   */
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/delete`, {});
  },

  /**
   * 清除未读消息数
   */
  clearUnreadMessageCount: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/clear-unread-message-count`,
      {}
    );
  },

  /**
   * 获取会话文件列表
   */
  getSessionFiles: (sessionId: string): Promise<SessionFile[]> => {
    return get<SessionFile[]>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/files`);
  },

  /**
   * 查看沙箱文件内容
   */
  viewFile: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ViewFileParams
  ): Promise<{ content: string; [key: string]: unknown }> => {
    return post<{ content: string; [key: string]: unknown }>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/file`,
      params
    );
  },

  /**
   * 查看 Shell 输出
   */
  viewShell: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ViewShellParams
  ): Promise<{ output: string; [key: string]: unknown }> => {
    return post<{ output: string; [key: string]: unknown }>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/shell`,
      params
    );
  },
};


```

Now let me rewrite the `useSessions` hook to use SSE for real-time updates:

```typescript
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {sessionApi} from '@/lib/api'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

/** 重连配置 */
const RETRY_CONFIG = {
  /** 最大重试次数 */
  maxRetries: 10,
  /** 基础延迟（毫秒） */
  baseDelay: 1000,
  /** 最大延迟（毫秒） */
  maxDelay: 30_000,
} as const

type UseSessionsReturn = {
  sessions: Session[]
  loading: boolean
  error: string | null
  /** 手动刷新（会通过 REST 接口拉取一次） */
  refresh: () => Promise<void>
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<boolean>
}

/**
 * 从 API 返回值中安全提取 Session 数组
 * 兼容 data 为 { sessions: [...] } / 直接数组 / null 等格式
 */
function normalizeSessions(raw: unknown): Session[] {
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as Session[]
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && 'sessions' in raw) {
    return Array.isArray((raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions)
      ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions as Session[]
      : []
  }
  return []
}

/**
 * 会话列表管理 Hook
 *
 * 通过 SSE 长连接 (/sessions/stream) 实时接收会话列表更新，
 * 无需每次展开侧栏时重新请求 /sessions。
 *
 * 连接断开时自动使用指数退避策略重连。
 * `refresh()` 方法可手动通过 REST 拉取一次数据。
 */
export function useSessions(): UseSessionsReturn {
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<Session[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  // 保存清理函数和重连定时器的引用，便于在组件卸载时统一清理
  const cleanupRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null)
  const retryTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)

  // ==================== 手动刷新（REST 回退） ====================
  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      const raw = await sessionApi.getSessions()
      const list = normalizeSessions(raw)
      setSessions(list)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[useSessions] 获取会话列表失败:', err)
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '获取会话列表失败')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  // ==================== SSE 实时订阅 ====================
  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true
    let retryCount = 0

    /**
     * 建立 SSE 连接
     * 每次调用会先清理旧连接
     */
    const connect = () => {
      if (!mounted) return

      // 清理上一次连接（如果有）
      if (cleanupRef.current) {
        cleanupRef.current()
        cleanupRef.current = null
      }

      const cleanup = sessionApi.streamSessions(
        // ---- onSessions ----
        (newSessions) => {
          if (!mounted) return
          // 收到有效数据，重置重试计数
          retryCount = 0
          setSessions(newSessions)
          setLoading(false)
          setError(null)
        },
        // ---- onError / onEnd ----
        (err) => {
          if (!mounted) return
          console.warn('[useSessions] SSE 连接断开:', err.message)

          if (retryCount >= RETRY_CONFIG.maxRetries) {
            setError('实时连接失败，请刷新页面')
            setLoading(false)
            return
          }

          // 指数退避重连
          const delay = Math.min(
            RETRY_CONFIG.baseDelay * Math.pow(2, retryCount),
            RETRY_CONFIG.maxDelay,
          )
          retryCount++
          console.log(
            `[useSessions] ${delay}ms 后尝试重连（第 ${retryCount} 次）`,
          )
          retryTimerRef.current = setTimeout(connect, delay)
        },
      )

      cleanupRef.current = cleanup
    }

    // 启动连接
    connect()

    // 组件卸载时清理
    return () => {
      mounted = false
      if (cleanupRef.current) {
        cleanupRef.current()
        cleanupRef.current = null
      }
      if (retryTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(retryTimerRef.current)
        retryTimerRef.current = null
      }
    }
  }, [])

  // ==================== 删除会话 ====================
  const deleteSession = useCallback(async (sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    try {
      await sessionApi.deleteSession(sessionId)
      // 乐观更新：立即从列表中移除
      // 下一次 SSE 推送会带来服务端的最终状态
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.session_id !== sessionId))
      return true
    } catch {
      return false
    }
  }, [])

  return {
    sessions,
    loading,
    error,
    refresh,
    deleteSession,
  }
}

```

Let me also verify the final state of the modified files:

已完成对接。以下是改动概述：

---

### 改动内容

**1. `src/lib/api/session.ts` — 重写 `streamSessions`**

- 将回调类型从通用的 `SSEEventHandler`（对应 `message`/`plan`/`step` 等事件）改为专用的 `SessionsStreamCallback`，即 `(sessions: Session[]) => void`
- 事件解析逻辑只处理 `event: sessions` 类型，从 `data.sessions` 中提取会话数组
- 当流正常结束（服务端关闭连接）时，也会触发 `onError` 回调，以便上层执行重连

**2. `src/hooks/use-sessions.ts` — SSE 实时订阅**

核心变化：**不再在每次展开侧栏时通过 REST `/sessions` 拉取数据**，而是组件挂载时建立 SSE 长连接，实时接收服务端推送的会话列表：

- **首次加载**：SSE 连接建立后服务端立即推送第一帧数据，`loading` 即被设为 `false`
- **实时更新**：后台任务状态变化、新增/完成会话时，服务端推送最新列表，UI 自动刷新
- **断线重连**：连接异常时采用**指数退避**策略自动重连（基础延迟 1s，最大 30s，最多重试 10 次）
- **手动刷新**：保留 `refresh()` 方法，可通过 REST 接口一次性拉取数据（用于错误态的"重试"按钮）
- **乐观更新**：删除会话时立即从列表中移除，无需等待下次 SSE 推送
- **清理机制**：组件卸载时自动关闭 SSE 连接并清除重连定时器，避免内存泄漏

**3. `session-list.tsx` — 无需改动**

`useSessions` 的返回接口（`sessions`, `loading`, `error`, `refresh`, `deleteSession`）保持不变，组件层无感知升级。

Let me check if there's any proxy configuration and write a quick test:

I found the root cause. The current `useSessions` hook relies **entirely** on SSE for data - if the SSE stream has any issue (CORS, parsing, etc.), no sessions ever display. The correct approach is to **load initial data via REST first, then use SSE for live updates**. Let me fix this:

```typescript
'use client'

import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {sessionApi} from '@/lib/api'
import type {Session} from '@/lib/api'

/** 重连配置 */
const RETRY_CONFIG = {
  /** 最大重试次数 */
  maxRetries: 10,
  /** 基础延迟（毫秒） */
  baseDelay: 1000,
  /** 最大延迟（毫秒） */
  maxDelay: 30_000,
} as const

type UseSessionsReturn = {
  sessions: Session[]
  loading: boolean
  error: string | null
  /** 手动刷新（会通过 REST 接口拉取一次） */
  refresh: () => Promise<void>
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<boolean>
}

/**
 * 从 API 返回值中安全提取 Session 数组
 * 兼容 data 为 { sessions: [...] } / 直接数组 / null 等格式
 */
function normalizeSessions(raw: unknown): Session[] {
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw as Session[]
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && 'sessions' in raw) {
    return Array.isArray((raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions)
      ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>).sessions as Session[]
      : []
  }
  return []
}

/**
 * 会话列表管理 Hook
 *
 * 1. 首次挂载时通过 REST (/sessions) 获取初始数据，确保列表立即可见
 * 2. 随后建立 SSE 长连接 (/sessions/stream) 实时接收会话列表更新
 * 3. 连接断开时自动使用指数退避策略重连
 * 4. `refresh()` 方法可手动通过 REST 拉取一次数据
 */
export function useSessions(): UseSessionsReturn {
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<Session[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  // 保存 SSE 清理函数和重连定时器的引用
  const cleanupRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null)
  const retryTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)

  // ==================== 通过 REST 获取会话列表 ====================
  const fetchSessions = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      setError(null)
      const raw = await sessionApi.getSessions()
      const list = normalizeSessions(raw)
      setSessions(list)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[useSessions] 获取会话列表失败:', err)
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '获取会话列表失败')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }, [])

  // ==================== 初始加载 + SSE 实时订阅 ====================
  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true
    let retryCount = 0

    // ---------- 1. 先通过 REST 获取初始数据 ----------
    sessionApi
      .getSessions()
      .then((raw) => {
        if (!mounted) return
        const list = normalizeSessions(raw)
        setSessions(list)
        setLoading(false)
        setError(null)
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!mounted) return
        console.error('[useSessions] 初始获取会话列表失败:', err)
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : '获取会话列表失败')
        setLoading(false)
      })

    // ---------- 2. 建立 SSE 长连接进行实时更新 ----------
    const connect = () => {
      if (!mounted) return

      // 清理上一次连接
      if (cleanupRef.current) {
        cleanupRef.current()
        cleanupRef.current = null
      }

      const cleanup = sessionApi.streamSessions(
        // ---- onSessions: 收到新的会话列表 ----
        (newSessions) => {
          if (!mounted) return
          retryCount = 0
          setSessions(newSessions)
          setLoading(false)
          setError(null)
        },
        // ---- onError / onEnd: 连接异常或流结束 ----
        (err) => {
          if (!mounted) return
          console.warn('[useSessions] SSE 连接断开:', err.message)

          if (retryCount >= RETRY_CONFIG.maxRetries) {
            // 超过最大重试次数，不再重连；列表数据仍保留
            console.error('[useSessions] 超过最大重试次数，停止重连')
            return
          }

          // 指数退避重连
          const delay = Math.min(
            RETRY_CONFIG.baseDelay * Math.pow(2, retryCount),
            RETRY_CONFIG.maxDelay,
          )
          retryCount++
          console.log(
            `[useSessions] ${delay}ms 后尝试重连（第 ${retryCount} 次）`,
          )
          retryTimerRef.current = setTimeout(connect, delay)
        },
      )

      cleanupRef.current = cleanup
    }

    // 启动 SSE 连接
    connect()

    // 组件卸载时清理
    return () => {
      mounted = false
      if (cleanupRef.current) {
        cleanupRef.current()
        cleanupRef.current = null
      }
      if (retryTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(retryTimerRef.current)
        retryTimerRef.current = null
      }
    }
  }, [])

  // ==================== 删除会话 ====================
  const deleteSession = useCallback(async (sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    try {
      await sessionApi.deleteSession(sessionId)
      // 乐观更新：立即从列表中移除
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.session_id !== sessionId))
      return true
    } catch {
      return false
    }
  }, [])

  return {
    sessions,
    loading,
    error,
    refresh: fetchSessions,
    deleteSession,
  }
}

```

Now let me also fix a potential issue in `streamSessions` where `stream.cancel()` on a locked stream could cause an unhandled promise rejection:

```typescript
import { get, post, createSSEStream, parseSSEStream } from "./fetch";
import type {
  Session,
  SessionsData,
  CreateSessionParams,
  ChatParams,
  SessionFile,
  ViewFileParams,
  ViewShellParams,
  SSEEventData,
  SSEEventHandler,
} from "./types";

/**
 * 会话列表流式更新回调
 */
type SessionsStreamCallback = (sessions: Session[]) => void;

/**
 * 会话模块 API
 */
export const sessionApi = {
  /**
   * 获取会话列表
   */
  getSessions: (): Promise<SessionsData> => {
    return get<SessionsData>("/sessions");
  },

  /**
   * 创建新会话
   */
  createSession: (params?: CreateSessionParams): Promise<Session> => {
    return post<Session>("/sessions", params || {});
  },

  /**
   * 流式订阅会话列表更新（SSE）
   *
   * 通过 /sessions/stream 端点建立 SSE 长连接，
   * 服务端定期推送完整的会话列表数据。
   *
   * @param onSessions 每次收到新会话列表时的回调
   * @param onError    连接异常或流结束时的回调（可用于触发重连）
   * @returns 清理函数，调用后会关闭连接
   */
  streamSessions: (
    onSessions: SessionsStreamCallback,
    onError?: (error: Error) => void
  ): (() => void) => {
    let aborted = false;
    let stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array> | null = null;

    const startStream = async () => {
      try {
        stream = await createSSEStream("/sessions/stream", {});

        await parseSSEStream(
          stream,
          (messageEvent) => {
            if (aborted) return;

            const data =
              typeof messageEvent.data === "string"
                ? JSON.parse(messageEvent.data)
                : messageEvent.data;

            // 服务端事件格式: event: sessions  data: { sessions: [...] }
            if (
              messageEvent.type === "sessions" &&
              data?.sessions &&
              Array.isArray(data.sessions)
            ) {
              onSessions(data.sessions as Session[]);
            }
          },
          (error) => {
            if (!aborted && onError) {
              onError(error);
            }
          }
        );

        // 流正常结束（服务端关闭连接），通知上层以便重连
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(new Error("SSE 流已结束"));
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(
            error instanceof Error ? error : new Error("SSE 连接失败")
          );
        }
      }
    };

    startStream();

    // 返回清理函数
    return () => {
      aborted = true;
      if (stream) {
        // stream 可能被 reader 锁定，cancel 会返回 rejected promise
        stream.cancel().catch(() => {});
      }
    };
  },

  /**
   * 获取会话详情
   */
  getSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<Session> => {
    return get<Session>(`/sessions/${sessionId}`);
  },

  /**
   * 发起聊天请求（SSE 流式）
   * @param sessionId 会话 ID
   * @param params 聊天参数
   * @param onEvent 事件处理器
   * @param onError 错误处理器
   * @returns 清理函数
   */
  chat: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ChatParams,
    onEvent: SSEEventHandler,
    onError?: (error: Error) => void
  ): (() => void) => {
    let aborted = false;
    let stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array> | null = null;

    const startStream = async () => {
      try {
        stream = await createSSEStream(`/sessions/${sessionId}/chat`, params);
        
        await parseSSEStream(
          stream,
          (messageEvent) => {
            if (aborted) return;
            
            // messageEvent.data 已经在 parseSSEStream 中解析为对象
            const data =
              typeof messageEvent.data === "string"
                ? JSON.parse(messageEvent.data)
                : messageEvent.data;
            
            onEvent({
              type: messageEvent.type as SSEEventData["type"],
              data,
            } as SSEEventData);
          },
          (error) => {
            if (!aborted && onError) {
              onError(error);
            }
          }
        );
      } catch (error) {
        if (!aborted && onError) {
          onError(
            error instanceof Error ? error : new Error("启动聊天流失败")
          );
        }
      }
    };

    startStream();

    // 返回清理函数
    return () => {
      aborted = true;
      if (stream) {
        stream.cancel();
      }
    };
  },

  /**
   * 停止会话
   */
  stopSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/stop`, {});
  },

  /**
   * 删除会话
   */
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/delete`, {});
  },

  /**
   * 清除未读消息数
   */
  clearUnreadMessageCount: (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
    return post<void>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/clear-unread-message-count`,
      {}
    );
  },

  /**
   * 获取会话文件列表
   */
  getSessionFiles: (sessionId: string): Promise<SessionFile[]> => {
    return get<SessionFile[]>(`/sessions/${sessionId}/files`);
  },

  /**
   * 查看沙箱文件内容
   */
  viewFile: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ViewFileParams
  ): Promise<{ content: string; [key: string]: unknown }> => {
    return post<{ content: string; [key: string]: unknown }>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/file`,
      params
    );
  },

  /**
   * 查看 Shell 输出
   */
  viewShell: (
    sessionId: string,
    params: ViewShellParams
  ): Promise<{ output: string; [key: string]: unknown }> => {
    return post<{ output: string; [key: string]: unknown }>(
      `/sessions/${sessionId}/shell`,
      params
    );
  },
};


```

Now let me verify the final state of both files to make sure everything is correct:

已修正。问题原因是之前把数据加载完全依赖了 SSE 流式接口，如果 SSE 连接有任何问题（跨域、解析异常等），`onSessions` 回调永远不会触发，导致组件一直停留在 `loading` 状态，会话列表无法显示。

### 修改内容

**`src/hooks/use-sessions.ts`** — 采用「REST 首屏 + SSE 实时更新」的双保险策略：

1. **首屏加载**：组件挂载时先通过 REST 接口 `GET /sessions` 拉取一次数据，确保会话列表立即可见
2. **SSE 实时更新**：同时建立 `/sessions/stream` 的 SSE 长连接，后续会话状态变化由服务端推送自动更新 UI
3. **SSE 失败降级**：即使 SSE 连接失败或断开，用户已有的列表数据不会丢失（不再设置 error 状态），仅在后台做指数退避重连
4. **手动刷新**：`refresh()` 仍可通过 REST 接口一次性拉取最新数据

**`src/lib/api/session.ts`** — 修复流关闭时的潜在异常：

- `stream.cancel()` 在 stream 被 reader 锁定时会返回 rejected promise，添加了 `.catch(() => {})` 防止未处理的 promise 拒绝

**User**

目前已经可以正常显示了，但是还是存在几个问题：1. 当一个任务的状态以及信息发生变化的时候（status和latest_message），任务会话列表不会有变化，流式事件接口返回的数据新增时也需要确保任务会话列表有新增；2. 页面发起了多次/sessions和/sessions/stream接口，非常消耗性能，请优化，确保仅发起一次请求。
